- One hundred twenty-eighth adapter increment complete: package-local runtime bootstrapping now lives in `runtimeBootstrap.ts` instead of being assembled directly in the `PackageLocalSessionRuntime` constructor. Focused tests prove initial state derivation and port resolution can be exercised together without constructing a session runtime, while topology guards keep the runtime class on the bootstrap seam instead of directly calling initial-state and noop-port factories.
- One hundred twenty-ninth adapter increment complete: package-local runtime port-field projection now lives in `runtimePortProjection.ts` instead of expanding resolved ports directly inside the `PackageLocalSessionRuntime` constructor. Focused tests prove resolved ports plus option-provided custom and builtin tool adapters can be projected without constructing a session runtime, while topology guards keep the constructor from reintroducing direct resolved-port field assignment.
- One hundred thirtieth adapter increment complete: package-local session/MCP connection operations now live in `runtimeConnectionOperations.ts` instead of being separately wired inside the `PackageLocalSessionRuntime` constructor. Focused tests prove session lifecycle close delegates MCP shutdown, while MCP tool refresh and configured-server access remain available through the same injected ports without constructing a session runtime.
- One hundred thirty-first adapter increment complete: runtime-independent token budget support now lives in `@blade-ai/agent` and flows through the session-first SDK. `AgentKernel` records model usage into an injected `TokenBudget`, emits `budget_warning` / `budget_exhausted` events, `PackageLocalSessionRuntime` creates one budget from `SessionOptions.tokenBudget` and reuses it across created kernels, and the package-local stream projection exposes those budget events as typed `StreamMessage` objects.
- One hundred thirty-second adapter increment complete: `@blade-ai/agent` now publishes `@blade-ai/agent/budget` as an explicit Pi-style subpath for `TokenBudget`, `TokenBudgetConfig`, and `TokenBudgetSnapshot`. Topology and packed-package consumer gates require the budget JS/declaration artifacts and verify both runtime and type imports from the subpath.
- One hundred thirty-third adapter increment complete: session-first SDK public types now reuse `TokenBudgetConfig` and `TokenBudgetSnapshot` from `@blade-ai/agent/budget` instead of duplicating the config shape or importing budget snapshots from the agent root. Root, package-local, build, and public declaration tsconfig path maps now include the budget subpath so local type-checking and declaration overlay builds resolve the same public boundary as consumers.
- One hundred thirty-fourth adapter increment complete: root legacy loop adapters now consume package-local session turn/tool/stream helpers through the explicit `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session/internal` migration subpath instead of deep-linking into `packages/agent-sdk/src/session`. The internal subpath is built, declared, browser-stubbed, and verified in packed temporary-consumer imports while topology keeps the root compatibility layer from traversing package source.
- One hundred thirty-fifth adapter increment complete: legacy session kernel adapters now consume `@blade-ai/agent` contracts through explicit Pi-style subpaths (`ports`, `protocol`, `state`, and `tracing`) instead of the agent root barrel. A monorepo topology guard keeps `SessionKernelAdapter`, `SessionKernelStoreAdapter`, `SessionKernelTraceAdapter`, and `SessionKernelHookAdapter` on those published package boundaries while the remaining legacy session layer is migrated.
- One hundred thirty-sixth adapter increment complete: package-local session runtime internals now consume `@blade-ai/agent` through explicit package subpaths (`kernel`, `loop`, `ports`, `protocol`, `state`, `tracing`, `budget`, and `epoch`) instead of the agent root barrel. The `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` source, build, and public declaration tsconfig maps include those subpaths, and topology guards keep package-local session runtime files on the same public boundaries that packed consumers use.
- One hundred thirty-seventh adapter increment complete: the remaining root legacy session runtime and kernel adapter tests now consume `@blade-ai/agent` through explicit package subpaths instead of the agent root barrel. The root tsconfig maps `kernel`, `ports`, `protocol`, and `tracing` to package source alongside the existing loop/state/epoch mappings, keeping root compatibility code on the same public package boundaries during the final migration.
- One hundred thirty-eighth adapter increment complete: package-local runtime tests now consume `@blade-ai/agent` through explicit package subpaths instead of the agent root barrel. Topology keeps kernel/runtime test coverage aligned with the same public boundaries used by package-local session implementation while preserving user-facing root facade examples.
- One hundred thirty-ninth adapter increment complete: package-local runtime token budget injection now lives in `runtimeTokenBudget.ts` instead of on `PackageLocalSessionRuntime`. The runtime class delegates session `tokenBudget` construction and kernel option injection through an operations seam, while focused tests and topology guards preserve explicit kernel budgets and prevent inline `TokenBudget` construction from drifting back into the class.
- One hundred fortieth adapter increment complete: package-local agent kernel factory typing now depends on the `runtimeAgentKernels.ts` port contract instead of importing from `runtimeInstance.ts`. This keeps the lightweight `kernelFactory.ts` adapter independent of the session runtime class while topology guards preserve the direct `@blade-ai/agent/kernel` construction boundary.
- One hundred forty-first adapter increment complete: root integration tests now import local MCP helpers through the public `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/local` subpath instead of deep-linking into `packages/agent-sdk/src/local`. The root TypeScript path map resolves that subpath to package-local source during local verification, and topology guards keep integration coverage aligned with publishable SDK boundaries.
- One hundred forty-second adapter increment complete: root package entrypoint contract tests now import the SDK facade through public `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/server` subpaths instead of traversing into `packages/agent-sdk/src`. Root TypeScript and Vitest resolution maps now route those public subpaths to package-local source during local verification, and topology guards keep entrypoint parity checks aligned with publishable package boundaries.
- One hundred forty-third adapter increment complete: the migration-only `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session/internal` entry now uses explicit exports for the root legacy loop adapter functions and types instead of wildcard-forwarding package-local session modules. Topology guards keep the internal entry narrow while preserving the temporary root compatibility bridge on a publishable subpath.
- One hundred forty-fourth adapter increment complete: `pnpm run verify:boundaries` now restricts root source imports from the migration-only `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session/internal` subpath to the current legacy loop adapter allowlist. A focused verifier fixture proves new root consumers are rejected, keeping the temporary compatibility bridge from spreading while the remaining root loop adapters are retired.
- One hundred forty-fifth adapter increment complete: root `executeToolCalls()` now owns its small batch sequencing wrapper locally by delegating each tool call through root `runToolCall()` instead of importing `executePackageLocalToolCalls` from the migration-only `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session/internal` subpath. The session-internal allowlist and topology guard were tightened so this retired root adapter cannot regress back onto the temporary compatibility bridge.
- One hundred forty-sixth adapter increment complete: root `streamChatResponse()` was first isolated from the migration-only `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session/internal` subpath, and the obsolete root path has since been retired. The internal entry no longer re-exports the package-local stream helper, and boundary/topology guards keep stream collection owned by the package-local session runtime.
- One hundred forty-seventh adapter increment complete: the migration-only `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session/internal` entry no longer re-exports `executePackageLocalToolCalls` or its input type after the root batch adapter retired that bridge. Topology now requires the internal entry to expose only the remaining root compatibility hooks for turn execution, single tool calls, and streaming tool execution.
- One hundred forty-eighth adapter increment complete: root `emitToolExecutionUpdate()` now owns the thin hook-dispatch compatibility wrapper locally instead of importing `emitPackageLocalToolExecutionUpdate` through the migration-only `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session/internal` subpath. The internal entry no longer re-exports the package-local update emitter or hook type, leaving single-tool execution as the only root tool-call helper still bridged through the temporary internal subpath.
- One hundred forty-ninth adapter increment complete: the migration-only `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session/internal` entry no longer re-exports the single-tool `PackageLocalRunToolCallInput` type. Root `runToolCall()` now derives that adapter input from the imported `runPackageLocalToolCall()` function signature locally, keeping package-local tool execution ownership unchanged while shrinking the explicit internal subpath surface.
- One hundred fiftieth adapter increment complete: the migration-only `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session/internal` entry no longer re-exports the root turn bridge event, input, hook, or outcome types. Root `runTurn()` derives its adapter input and async-generator event/outcome types from the imported `runPackageLocalTurn()` function signature locally, preserving the package-local turn execution owner while narrowing the explicit internal subpath to function/class runtime bridges.
- One hundred fifty-first adapter increment complete: the migration-only `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session/internal` entry no longer re-exports `PackageLocalStreamingToolExecutorConfig`. The root `StreamingToolExecutor` wrapper derives the delegate config parameter from `PackageLocalStreamingToolExecutor.collectAndExecute()` locally, keeping the temporary bridge class available while removing another explicit package-local type from the internal subpath contract.
- One hundred fifty-second adapter increment complete: the unused root `StreamingToolExecutor` compatibility wrapper has been retired, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session/internal` no longer re-exports `PackageLocalStreamingToolExecutor`. Root source now consumes the migration-only internal subpath only from `runToolCall()` and `runTurn()`, while package-local streaming tool execution remains owned by `packages/agent-sdk/src/session/streamingToolExecutor.ts` through relative package-local imports.
- One hundred fifty-third adapter increment complete: at that migration stage, `pnpm run verify:boundaries` restricted the then-two remaining root `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session/internal` consumers by imported bridge name, not just file path. `src/agent/loop/runToolCall.ts` could import only `runPackageLocalToolCall`, and `src/agent/loop/runTurn.ts` could import only `runPackageLocalTurn`, so the temporary internal subpath could not silently grow wider through an already-allowlisted root adapter; later retirement increments consolidate both bridges into `rootAgentLoopAdapter.ts`.

### Phase 5: Production Verification Chain

Objective: Make quality gates broad enough for a production agent SDK.

Deliverables:

- `pnpm run verify`
- `pnpm run verify:packages`
- `pnpm run verify:entrypoints`
- `pnpm run test:unit`
- `pnpm run test:integration`
- `pnpm run test:live:glm`
- temporary consumer install test from packed tarballs
- package boundary scanner
- browser bundle scanner
- documentation build gate

CI:

- CI runs lint, type-check, build, unit, integration, package verification, docs build.
- Live GLM tests stay manual or scheduled with secrets, not mandatory for every PR.

Status:

- First verification-chain increment complete: root `pnpm run verify` now aggregates lint, root and workspace type checks, package boundary checks, docs build, entrypoint/browser-safety checks, packed package smoke tests, unit tests, and integration tests. `pnpm run verify:packages` packs `@blade-ai/ai`, `@blade-ai/agent`, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk`, checks required JS/declaration files and absence of test files, installs the tarballs into an external temporary consumer, and imports the public package/subpath exports. The release workflow now runs the same `pnpm run verify` gate before semantic-release.
- Second verification-chain increment complete: the package boundary verifier now rejects forbidden runtime dependencies declared in the `@blade-ai/agent` manifest, not just forbidden imports from source files, protecting the runtime-independent kernel boundary before code is imported.
- Third verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run test:live:session-glm` now provides a dedicated session-first live smoke over the built `@blade-ai/ai`, `@blade-ai/agent`, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` packages, while the heavier `test:integration` suite remains explicitly gated by `INTEGRATION_API_KEY` / `INTEGRATION_BASE_URL`.
- Fourth verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now inspects packed declaration file contents for critical ownership regressions, currently preventing `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session` from publishing a `dist/session/index.d.ts` that points back at the legacy root `./Session.js` declarations.
- Fifth verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now fresh-builds `@blade-ai/ai`, `@blade-ai/agent`, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` before packing, and it rejects `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/tools` declaration regressions that point back at legacy root `./core/createTool.js` or `./catalog/index.js` declarations.
- Sixth verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now rejects root `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` declaration overlays that leak temporary `public-index.js` references or legacy root implementation declarations, and it rejects `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/local` declaration regressions that point back at root MCP, memory, sandbox, or builtin-tool declaration modules.
- Seventh verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now requires the packed `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` permission declarations, rejects public declaration maps for overlaid root/core/local/session/tools/permission entries, and prevents permission declarations from regressing to legacy `SensitiveFileDetector` or `ToolEffects` dependencies.
- Eighth verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now rejects packed `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/tools` runtime and declaration regressions that point back at legacy root `src/tools/core/createTool` or `src/tools/catalog/ToolCatalog`, keeping the public tool authoring entry package-local.
- Ninth verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now rejects packed root `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` runtime and declaration regressions that point back at legacy root `src/agent/subagents`, keeping root subagent compatibility exports package-local.
- Tenth verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now requires the packed `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session` runtime factory declaration and rejects `dist/session/index.js` regressions that directly point back at legacy root `src/session/Session`, keeping the public session entry on the package-local factory seam.
- Eleventh verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now rejects packed `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/local` entry regressions where `dist/local/index.js` directly points back at legacy root `src/mcp`, `src/memory`, `src/sandbox`, or `src/tools/builtin`, while topology tests require package-local MCP, memory, sandbox, and builtin-tool adapter source files.
- Twelfth verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now rejects packed `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/local` memory declaration regressions that publish the earlier stub-style `read`/`write`/boolean-delete API instead of the package-local `save`/`get`/`list`/`delete` memory API.
- Thirteenth verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now require package-local memory tool sources and reject legacy root memory-tool imports from `local/builtin-tools.ts`; package verification also rejects packed `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/local` runtime regressions that point directly at legacy root memory tools.
- Fourteenth verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now reject legacy root sandbox imports from `local/sandbox.ts`, and package verification rejects packed local sandbox declarations that fall back to the old placeholder constructor-only shape.
- Fifteenth verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now reject legacy root MCP imports from `local/mcp.ts`, and package verification rejects packed local MCP declarations that fall back to the old `unknown[]` placeholder signatures.
- Sixteenth verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now reject legacy root builtin-tool imports from `local/builtin-tools.ts`, and package verification rejects packed local builtin declarations that fall back to the old `getBuiltinTools(...args: unknown[])` placeholder signature.
- Seventeenth verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now require package-local session store source ownership, and package verification requires the packed `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` session store declaration while rejecting declaration regressions that point back at root context storage or legacy root `SessionStore`.
- Eighteenth verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now require package-local session config source ownership, and package verification requires the packed `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` session config declaration while rejecting declaration regressions that point back at legacy root `Session` or non-package-local config types.
- Nineteenth verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now require `packages/agent-sdk/src/session/Session.ts` and reject default factory imports that bypass it. Package verification also requires the packed `dist/session/Session.d.ts` declaration and rejects declarations that expose legacy root `src/session/Session` contracts.
- Twentieth verification-chain increment complete: session factory tests now prove package-local `prompt()` is composed from `create`/`send`/`stream`/`close` instead of delegating to `SessionRuntimeFactory.prompt()`, and topology tests reject reintroducing the root `prompt` import in the legacy session adapter.
- Twenty-first verification-chain increment complete: session factory tests now prove package-local `forkSession()` is composed from `resume` plus the live session's `fork()` and source-session cleanup instead of delegating to `SessionRuntimeFactory.fork()`, and topology tests reject reintroducing the root `forkSession` import in the legacy session adapter.
- Twenty-second verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now reject public `session/index.ts` regressions where `createSession()` or `resumeSession()` call `sessionRuntimeFactory.create/resume` directly instead of entering the package-local lifecycle facade.
- Twenty-third verification-chain increment complete: session factory tests now call package-local lifecycle functions directly and prove `resumeSession()`/`forkSession()` reject `persistSession: false` before touching the runtime factory. Topology tests also reject moving those guard strings back into the public `session/index.ts` entry.
- Twenty-fourth verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now require `SessionRuntimeFactory` and the default runtime factory to stay limited to `create()`/`resume()` primitives, preventing future `fork()` or `prompt()` runtime hooks from bypassing package-local lifecycle ownership.
- Twenty-fifth verification-chain increment complete: package verification now rejects packed `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` session factory declarations that expose `fork(options)` or `prompt(message)`, so the published `SessionRuntimeFactory` contract remains a narrow `create()`/`resume()` primitive port.
- Twenty-sixth verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now reject static `legacySessionAdapter` imports from the default session runtime factory and require the adapter to be loaded through `import('./legacySessionAdapter.js')`, preserving a lazy boundary around the remaining root session implementation.
- Twenty-seventh verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now extracts the packed `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` tarball, walks the static import closure from `package/dist/session/index.js`, and rejects eager inclusion of legacy root session runtime markers while still permitting the temporary adapter to remain behind a dynamic `import()` boundary.
- Twenty-eighth verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now reject `as never` and `as unknown as ISession` in the temporary legacy session adapter, forcing the remaining bridge to stay typed and explicit while the package-local session implementation is migrated.
- Twenty-ninth verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now reject direct imports from legacy root `src/session/types.ts` inside the temporary session adapter, so adapter compatibility types must be inferred from the remaining legacy entrypoint rather than coupling to broader root session contracts.
- Thirtieth verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now reject direct imports from legacy root `src/types/branded.ts` inside the temporary session adapter, keeping the adapter's remaining root dependency focused on the legacy `Session` entrypoint.
- Thirty-first verification-chain increment complete: topology tests now extract every root `src/*` import from the temporary legacy session adapter and compare it to a single allowed import list, preventing future piecemeal legacy root dependencies from sneaking into the session bridge.
- Thirty-second verification-chain increment complete: focused package-local tests now cover session content helper behavior for string prompts, multimodal text/image parts, and JSON fallback parsing; topology also requires the helper source to remain package-local without root imports.
- Thirty-third verification-chain increment complete: focused package-local tests now cover pending-turn lifecycle behavior for missing sends, single-message consumption, overlapping send rejection, and cleanup. Topology also requires the pending-turn source to remain package-local without root imports.
- Thirty-fourth verification-chain increment complete: focused package-local tests now cover turn abort-scope behavior for local signals, external abort propagation, session abort propagation, cleanup listener disconnection, and overlapping turn rejection. Topology also requires the turn-abort source to remain package-local without root imports.
- Thirty-fifth verification-chain increment complete: focused package-local tests now cover prompt stream accumulation for successful results, tool result updates, stream errors, and result-error fallbacks. Topology also requires the prompt accumulator source to remain package-local without root imports and requires the package-local `prompt()` lifecycle to instantiate it.
- Thirty-sixth verification-chain increment complete: session factory lifecycle tests now prove prompt stream errors and fork errors remain the user-visible failure when cleanup also fails. Topology also requires the cleanup helper source to remain package-local without root imports and requires the package-local lifecycle to call it.
- Thirty-seventh verification-chain increment complete: focused package-local tests now cover session lifecycle state behavior for open/closed guards, close cleanup idempotence, pending-turn cleanup, and abort-without-close semantics. Topology also requires the lifecycle state source to remain package-local without root imports.
- Thirty-eighth verification-chain increment complete: focused package-local tests now cover turn preparation behavior for context snapshot merging, pending-turn consumption, active abort-scope creation, external abort propagation, and closed-session guards. Topology also requires the turn controller source to remain package-local without root imports.
- Thirty-ninth verification-chain increment complete: focused package-local tests now cover trace recorder behavior for unified stream/tool/usage/hook/result events, safe default redaction, explicit payload capture, and cloned trace reads. Topology also requires the recorder source to remain package-local without root imports.
- Fortieth verification-chain increment complete: focused package-local tests now cover session trace manager behavior for observability disabled mode, recorder creation with metadata and prompt events, bounded trace retention, and sink failure isolation. - Ninety-first verification-chain increment complete: integration tests now reuse the shared GLM live config loader so `.env`, `GLM_*`, and `INTEGRATION_*` aliases resolve through the same path as live smoke tests. - Ninety-second verification-chain increment complete: publishable package manifests now carry npm-facing metadata for license, engines, side-effect safety, homepage, bugs, and keywords, each package has its own README, and `pnpm run verify:packages` requires `package/README.md` inside every packed tarball before running the temporary-consumer install smoke.
- Packed package npm metadata gate complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now extracts each packed tarball and rejects package manifests whose `license`, `homepage`, `bugs`, or `repository` metadata drift from the source release contract before any npm publication can start. This mirrors the post-publish metadata gate while catching metadata loss at the pre-release tarball stage.
- Package description metadata artifact gate complete: source release verification, packed-package manifest checks, and post-publish temporary-consumer manifest checks now reject package descriptions that drift from the three public package roles: provider-agnostic model interfaces, runtime-independent agent kernel contracts, and the session-first SDK facade. The package description metadata artifact gate keeps npm search results and agent-readable package identity aligned with the Pi-style split.
- Package author metadata artifact gate complete: all three publishable package manifests now declare `author: "echoVic"`, and source release verification, packed-package manifest checks, and post-publish temporary-consumer manifest checks reject artifacts whose author metadata drifts. The package author metadata artifact gate keeps npm-visible maintainer identity consistent across source, tarballs, and installed packages.
- Package discoverability metadata artifact gate complete: packed-package and post-publish temporary-consumer manifest checks now reject npm artifacts whose keywords drop the baseline `agent`, `sdk`, or `llm` discoverability markers. The package discoverability metadata artifact gate keeps the npm-visible search contract aligned with the source release verifier after pack, publish, and install.
- Package engine metadata artifact gate complete: packed-package and post-publish temporary-consumer manifest checks now reject npm artifacts whose `engines.node` metadata drifts from `>=22.14.0`. The package engine metadata artifact gate keeps the Node runtime contract visible in both the tarballs semantic-release will publish and the installed packages external users consume.
- Package module metadata artifact gate complete: packed-package and post-publish temporary-consumer manifest checks now reject npm artifacts whose `type` metadata drifts from `module` or whose `sideEffects` metadata stops being `false`. The package module metadata artifact gate keeps the ESM-only and side-effect-free package contract visible in both pre-publication tarballs and installed npm artifacts.
- Package license artifact gate complete: each publishable package now includes its own `LICENSE` file, `pnpm run verify:release` requires `LICENSE` in the publish files whitelist, `pnpm run verify:packages` rejects tarballs missing `package/LICENSE` or the MIT permission grant, and `pnpm run verify:published -- --version <version>` rejects installed npm artifacts missing the same license text. The package license artifact gate makes the npm-visible legal contract explicit instead of relying only on manifest metadata.
- Packed package manifest entry target gate complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now extracts each packed tarball and rejects package manifest `main`, `types`, and `exports` targets that point outside `./dist/*` or the explicit `./package.json` metadata export. The packed package manifest entry target gate mirrors the post-publish manifest entry target gate while catching source-file, absolute-path, and package-escape regressions before publication.
- Packed SDK browser export condition gate complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now rejects packed `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` manifests whose root browser condition no longer resolves to `./dist/browser/index.js`, or whose `server`, `session`, `session/internal`, and `local` browser conditions no longer resolve to the explicit server-only stub. The packed SDK browser export condition gate catches browser-stub regressions before npm publication, complementing the temporary-consumer browser bundle smoke.
- Ninety-third verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now writes a TypeScript temporary-consumer file after installing the packed tarballs and runs `tsc --noEmit` against public `@blade-ai/ai`, `@blade-ai/agent`, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` imports, proving package declarations support real external ModelPort, AgentKernel, SessionOptions, StreamMessage, defineTool, and ToolKind usage.
- Ninety-fourth verification-chain increment complete: the packed temporary consumer now imports and type-checks the provider-focused `@blade-ai/ai/deepseek`, `@blade-ai/ai/providers/openai-compatible`, and `@blade-ai/ai/providers/vercel` subpaths, while tarball checks require their JS and declaration artifacts so exported provider contracts cannot regress silently.
- Ninety-fifth verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now browser-bundles a temporary consumer against the packed `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` tarball with `--platform=browser` and `--conditions=browser`, proving the root browser condition resolves to the server-only `createSession` stub while browser-safe `core` and `tools` contracts stay free of Node-only dependency markers.
- Ninety-sixth verification-chain increment complete: the packed browser-bundle consumer now also imports `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/session`, `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/server`, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/local` under browser conditions and executes their stubs, proving server/local/session subpaths fail clearly in browser bundles without dragging Node, MCP, shell, or local runtime dependencies into client output.

- Ninety-eighth verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now type-checks packed temporary-consumer imports across the public `@blade-ai/agent/kernel`, `protocol`, `ports`, `state`, and `tracing` subpaths, proving the runtime-independent agent kernel declarations are consumable directly instead of relying on root re-export coverage.
- Ninety-ninth verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now type-checks packed temporary-consumer imports across the public `@blade-ai/ai/chat`, `model`, and `retry` subpaths, proving chat compatibility contracts, provider-agnostic model contracts, and retry policy helpers are consumable directly instead of relying on root re-export coverage.
- One-hundredth verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:entrypoints` now runs its browser bundle smoke through the esbuild JS API instead of an external CLI subprocess, reducing process-wrapper variance while keeping the browser-condition bundle and Node-only marker checks intact.
- One-hundred-first verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now writes and executes a packed temporary-consumer `consumer-runtime.mjs` file that imports public runtime subpaths, asserts key value exports for `@blade-ai/ai`, `@blade-ai/agent`, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk`, and verifies type-only subpaths stay empty at runtime, proving published tarballs expose the intended Node ESM runtime surface rather than merely resolving module specifiers.

- One-hundred-third verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:boundaries` now rejects `@blade-ai/ai` manifest dependencies on `@blade-ai/agent` or `@blade-ai/agent-sdk`, proving provider-layer ownership cannot regress through package metadata before source imports are scanned.

- One-hundred-fifth verification-chain increment complete: all three publishable packages now expose `./package.json` metadata subpaths, and `pnpm run verify:packages` runtime-loads those metadata exports from packed temporary-consumer installs, proving package names and provenance metadata remain consumable after npm packing.
- One-hundred-sixth verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:boundaries` now rejects direct `ai` provider-runtime dependencies in the `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` manifest, and the publishable session SDK relies on `@blade-ai/ai` for provider execution ownership instead of declaring the provider runtime itself.

- One-hundred-thirteenth verification-chain increment complete: topology now requires obsolete root package build config files (`tsup.config.ts` and `tsconfig.build.json`) to be absent, keeping build ownership exclusively in the publishable package directories while root `pnpm run build` remains an orchestrator command.
- One-hundred-fourteenth verification-chain increment complete: `pnpm run verify:examples` now type-checks `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` examples against `packages/agent-sdk/src/index.ts` instead of the legacy root `src/index.ts`, proving quickstart examples exercise the publishable package facade during local verification.

- One-hundred-sixteenth verification-chain increment complete: the `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` public declaration overlay now resolves `@blade-ai/ai` public imports from built package declarations instead of inherited workspace source aliases, and topology forbids `../../src`, `../ai/src`, and `../agent/src` aliases in that overlay config.
- One-hundred-seventeenth verification-chain increment complete: the `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` package type-check program now includes only package-local `src/**/*` and no longer exposes the legacy `@/* -> ../../src/*` alias, proving package-local SDK source can type-check without treating the root implementation tree as part of the package.
- One-hundred-eighteenth verification-chain increment complete: the `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` declaration build now emits from package-local `src/**/*` instead of root `../../src/**/*`, resolves `@blade-ai/ai` and `@blade-ai/agent` imports from built declarations, and packed package verification forbids representative legacy root declaration directories such as `dist/agent`, `dist/context`, and `dist/mcp`.
- One-hundred-nineteenth verification-chain increment complete: all publishable package declaration builds now disable declaration maps, and `pnpm run verify:packages` dynamically rejects any packed `.d.ts.map` entry so npm tarballs keep their type surface concise and do not ship local source-map metadata.
- One-hundred-twentieth verification-chain increment complete: all publishable package JS builds now disable source maps, and `pnpm run verify:packages` dynamically rejects any packed `.js.map` entry so npm tarballs do not ship local JavaScript source-map metadata.
- One-hundred-twenty-first verification-chain increment complete: topology now guards the remaining root session source against migration-era stream runtime markers, rejecting `runtime?: 'kernel' | 'legacy'`, `experimentalKernel`, and explicit legacy/kernel branch checks in `src/session/types.ts` and `src/session/Session.ts` so the source contract stays aligned with the packed kernel-only session declarations.
- One-hundred-twenty-second verification-chain increment complete: browser-bundle verification now tolerates two consecutive transient esbuild service stops by default, and the post-publish browser bundle smoke reuses the same retry helper while still loading esbuild from the temporary npm consumer install.
- One-hundred-twenty-third verification-chain increment complete: the package-local `Session` instance now owns basic control state for `setPermissionMode()`, `setModel()`, and `setMaxTurns()` without requiring a legacy delegate. - One-hundred-twenty-fourth verification-chain increment complete: package-local kernel sessions now inject a `PackageLocalSessionRuntimePort` into `PackageLocalSession`, routing MCP status/list/actions through the package-local runtime instead of requiring a legacy delegate. - One-hundred-twenty-fifth verification-chain increment complete: `PackageLocalSession.fork()` now routes through the package-local runtime port before falling back to the migration delegate, and missing fork support returns an explicit capability error instead of a migration-era stub. - One-hundred-twenty-sixth verification-chain increment complete: the package-local JSONL session store can now materialize a fork snapshot into a new session file via `writeForkState()`, preserving forked message history and parent session metadata in the same event format consumed by `loadState()`. Focused tests prove `forkState()` output can be written and reloaded without using the legacy root session store.
- One-hundred-twenty-seventh verification-chain increment complete: the default package-local kernel runtime now wires `storagePath` to a package-local JSONL session store adapter and implements `fork()` by reading parent history, materializing a new child session file, and returning a new package-local session through the same runtime factory. Focused tests prove a resumed session can fork persisted JSONL history without using the legacy root session runtime.
- One-hundred-twenty-eighth verification-chain increment complete: the default package-local kernel store port now persists kernel-appended turn messages into the package-local JSONL session store instead of dropping them through a noop store. Focused tests prove a session created through the default kernel runtime writes user and assistant messages, kernel turn metadata, and reasoning content into history that `loadState()` can read back.
- One-hundred-twenty-ninth verification-chain increment complete: package-local create/resume lifecycle now runs through the default kernel runtime factory before returning sessions, calling `ensureSessionCreated()` for new sessions and `ensureSessionLoaded()` for resumed sessions. Focused tests prove empty created sessions and missing resumed sessions both materialize `session_created` JSONL state without sending a prompt or importing the legacy root session runtime.
- One-hundred-thirtieth verification-chain increment complete: package-local sessions now hydrate the synchronous `session.messages` view from the JSONL store during create/resume initialization. Package regressions cover tool declaration exposure, chained hook input/output rewrites, post-rewrite permission and path-safety evaluation, abort-before-execution behavior, thrown-tool failure hooks, execution, in-process MCP discovery/calls, concurrent connection sharing, unexpected transport closure, honest disabled/unknown MCP health projection, initialization-failure resource cleanup, tool events, and final continuation without injected ports. Opt-in `glm-5.2` scenarios have exercised custom tool call/result continuation, explicit `canUseTool` denial under YOLO, hook input/output mutation, in-process MCP invocation, and MCP disconnect/reconnect recovery through the publishable facade. The root session intentionally does not auto-register Node-local builtins; the `/local` baseline now provides package-owned `Read` plus opt-in `MemoryRead` / `MemoryWrite`, while Edit/Write/Bash/Task remain separate package-local migrations. OAuth token flow, periodic health monitoring, and advanced execution-pipeline features such as scheduling, file locking, tool timeouts, execution history, and result-artifact externalization also remain separate migration work and are not claimed by this increment. TypeScript/Vitest source maps, topology tests, root export behavior, browser stub behavior, package-local facade tests, and docs contracts guard the package-owned entrypoint boundary.
- Three-hundred-twenty-sixth local-tool composition increment complete: public `SessionOptions.tools` now accepts the explicit `SessionTool` union of `ToolDefinition` and prebuilt `Tool`. Package-local registration converts only definitions through `toolFromDefinition()` and registers prebuilt tools directly, so `getBuiltinTools()` from the Node-only `/local` entry can be passed straight into root `createSession({ tools })` without a second session facade or an exposed internal runtime provider. Both forms remain session-explicit `custom/workspace` sources rather than receiving an implicit `builtin/trusted` privilege upgrade. Focused RED/GREEN tests cover mixed definition/runtime registration, prebuilt-only registration without a factory, public type compatibility, source/trust classification, and a real default-kernel `MemoryRead` tool-call continuation. Shared local, packed, and post-publish TypeScript consumers now require root and `/session` `SessionTool` declarations and prove `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/tools` `Tool` values remain assignable, while browser-safe static boundaries remain unchanged because the root package still does not import `/local`.
- Three-hundred-twenty-seventh local Read-tool migration increment complete: Node-local file reading, the injectable filesystem port, and shared read-before-write `FileAccessTracker` now live under `packages/agent-sdk/src/local/file`. `getBuiltinTools()` returns the package-owned `Read` tool by default and appends memory tools only when a memory manager is provided; root session behavior remains explicit because callers still pass the returned tools through `SessionOptions.tools` and provide filesystem roots through runtime context. The legacy root Read and tracker modules are package forwarders, allowing the remaining root Edit/Write implementations to share the package-owned access records until they migrate. Focused tests cover text slicing, metadata, filesystem-capability denial, default builtin registration, root tracker compatibility, and topology ownership. Local, packed, published, and browser-condition verifiers cover `createReadTool`, `FileAccessTracker`, local file types, root/core exclusion, server-only browser failures, and rejection of legacy root filesystem imports.
- Three-hundred-twenty-eighth local Write-tool migration increment complete: Node-local file writing now lives under `packages/agent-sdk/src/local/file/write.ts`. `getBuiltinTools()` returns both the package-owned `Read` and `Write` tools by default. The `LocalFileSystemPort` interface was extended with `writeTextFile()` and `mkdir()` methods, and `createNodeLocalFileSystem()` provides real Node implementations. The legacy root `src/tools/builtin/file/write.ts` is now a package forwarder shim that re-exports `createWriteTool` from `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/local`. Focused tests cover text file creation, filesystem-capability denial, unread-file rejection, automatic directory creation, and default builtin registration. Topology tests verify the legacy root forwarder shape. Local exports expose `createWriteTool`, `writeTool`, and `WriteToolOptions`.

Commit:

- `ci: add production verification chain`

### Phase 6: Release Automation

Objective: Publish packages automatically from `main` when all gates pass.

Deliverables:

- semantic-release configured for monorepo packages.
- npm trusted publishing/provenance preserved.
- package changelogs or release notes generated per package.
- release workflow runs `pnpm run verify` before publishing.
- dry-run release workflow or command documented.

Status:

- First release-automation increment complete: semantic-release now uses a fixed-version monorepo release for `@blade-ai/ai`, `@blade-ai/agent`, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk`. The release workflow still runs `pnpm run verify` before publishing, uses GitHub OIDC trusted publishing without `NPM_TOKEN`, and publishes all three package roots through `@semantic-release/npm`. A local prepare plugin synchronizes each package manifest to `nextRelease.version` and rewrites internal `workspace:*` dependencies to the same concrete version before npm publish. `pnpm run release:dry` remains the tokened release rehearsal path without publishing.
- Second release-automation increment complete: release notes now include a generated per-package section for the fixed-version monorepo release, listing the published `@blade-ai/ai`, `@blade-ai/agent`, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` versions plus the session-first install command after the conventional commit notes.
- Third release-automation increment complete: the retired manual `scripts/release.js` path and its helper were removed, `release:legacy` is no longer exposed from the root package scripts, and `pnpm run verify:release` now rejects any reintroduction of manual release aliases or legacy release helper files so publishing stays on the semantic-release workflow from `main`.
- Fourth release-automation increment complete: the repository now pins the pnpm toolchain to `pnpm@11.7.0` in root package metadata and every GitHub workflow that installs dependencies, while release verification rejects unpinned release workflow pnpm setup so CI, docs, and main-branch publishing use the same package manager version.
- Fifth release-automation increment complete: `pnpm run verify:release` now copies the real publishable package manifests into a temporary release-prep workspace, runs the semantic-release workspace-version prepare plugin with a synthetic release version, and rejects prepared manifests that still contain `workspace:` or `0.0.0` placeholders so fixed-version monorepo publishing is verified against the actual package metadata.
- Sixth release-automation increment complete: all three publishable packages now declare `publishConfig.provenance: true`, and `pnpm run verify:release` rejects package metadata that would publish without explicit npm provenance enabled, complementing the GitHub OIDC trusted publishing workflow.
- Seventh release-automation increment complete: the repository now exposes `pnpm run verify:published -- --version <version>` as a post-publish visibility check that polls the GitHub Release and all three npm packages, so maintainers can verify public artifacts after the main-branch semantic-release workflow finishes.
- Eighth release-automation increment complete: `pnpm run verify:published -- --version <version>` now installs the released `@blade-ai/ai`, `@blade-ai/agent`, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` versions into a temporary consumer and runs runtime import smoke checks against the public package exports, proving the external npm install path works after publication.
- Ninth release-automation increment complete: the post-publish verifier now also writes a temporary TypeScript consumer and runs `tsc --noEmit` against public `ModelPort`, `AgentKernelOptions`, `SessionOptions`, `StreamMessage`, and tool declaration imports from the released npm packages, proving the external type surface works after publication.
- Tenth release-automation increment complete: the post-publish verifier now type-checks public subpath declarations from the released npm packages, including `@blade-ai/ai/model`, `@blade-ai/ai/providers/openai-compatible`, `@blade-ai/agent/kernel`, `@blade-ai/agent/ports`, `@blade-ai/agent/protocol`, and the session/tools/core `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` subpaths, proving direct Pi-style package-boundary imports work after publication.
- Eleventh release-automation increment complete: the main-branch release workflow now runs post-publish verification after semantic-release. It refreshes release tags, discovers the latest `v*` tag, and runs `pnpm run verify:published -- --version <tag>` with `GH_TOKEN`, so public GitHub Release, npm package visibility, runtime import smoke, and root/subpath TypeScript declaration checks are part of the automated publishing job instead of only a manual maintainer step.
- Twelfth release-automation increment complete: the post-publish verifier now inspects each released npm package's registry `dist.attestations.provenance.predicateType` and requires the SLSA provenance predicate (`https://slsa.dev/provenance/v1`), so npm provenance attestations are verified after publication rather than only configured in package metadata.
- Thirteenth release-automation increment complete: the release workflow now captures the latest `v*` tag before semantic-release and compares it with the latest tag after semantic-release. Post-publish verification runs only when a new release tag appears, preventing no-release main-branch pushes from re-verifying an older tag that may not match the current three-package publish contract.
- Fourteenth release-automation increment complete: the post-publish temporary consumer now runtime-loads and type-checks the published `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/server`, `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/local`, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/browser` subpaths in addition to root, `session`, `tools`, and `core`, keeping the released npm verification aligned with the packed package boundary smoke for server-only, Node-local, and browser-safe entrypoints.
- Fifteenth release-automation increment complete: the post-publish temporary consumer now installs `esbuild`, browser-bundles the released SDK root plus `session`, `server`, `local`, `core`, and `tools` subpaths with the browser condition, executes the resulting bundle, and scans for Node-only markers, proving the published npm artifacts preserve the same browser-safe/server-only boundary verified by packed tarball checks.
- Sixteenth release-automation increment complete: `pnpm run verify:release` now rejects publishable package manifests that are not ESM-only or side-effect-free. Focused release verifier tests prove all three public packages must keep `type: "module"` and `sideEffects: false`, preserving the package shape expected by modern Node, bundlers, and temporary-consumer package checks.
- Seventeenth release-automation increment complete: `pnpm run verify:release` now rejects widened publishable package `files` metadata. Focused release verifier tests prove `@blade-ai/ai` and `@blade-ai/agent` publish only `dist` plus `README.md`, while `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` may additionally publish `vendor/ripgrep/**`, keeping npm tarball scope narrow before pack/install verification runs.
- Supply-chain release-automation increment complete: CI, docs, and release workflows now install dependencies with `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile --ignore-scripts`, and `pnpm run verify:release` rejects workflow dependency installs that omit the frozen lockfile or lifecycle-script suppression. This matches the Pi-style supply-chain hardening posture while keeping release publishing on trusted npm provenance.
- Supply-chain exact-dependency increment complete: root and publishable package manifests now use exact versions for direct external dependencies, with internal `@blade-ai/*` workspace links remaining as `workspace:*` until release prepare rewrites them to the concrete fixed-version release. `pnpm run verify:release` rejects future direct dependency ranges such as `^` or `~`, reducing supply-chain drift before CI, pack, and post-publish consumer checks run.
- Supply-chain build-script allowlist increment complete: `pnpm-workspace.yaml` keeps dependency build scripts behind a three-package allowlist (`@vscode/ripgrep`, `esbuild`, and `node-pty`), and `pnpm run verify:release` rejects any wider allowlist before CI or main-branch release can run. This complements `--ignore-scripts` installs by making any approved dependency build scripts explicit and reviewable.

- Nineteenth release-automation increment complete: `pnpm run verify:release` now rejects release workflows whose `actions/setup-node` version drifts below the package engine floor. Focused workflow tests prove the main-branch publishing job stays pinned to Node `22.14`, matching the `>=22.14.0` engine contract used by all three publishable packages.

- source manifest target relativity gate complete: `pnpm run verify:boundaries` now rejects source package manifest `main`, `types`, and export condition targets that omit the package-relative `./` prefix, mirroring packed and post-publish manifest target shape checks before build artifacts are packed.

- packed package dependency-version gate complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now rejects external dependency ranges and `0.0.0` placeholders in packed tarball manifests. Internal `@blade-ai/*` local pack placeholders remain covered by release prepare and post-publish manifest checks, while npm-facing external dependencies are pinned before artifacts reach semantic-release.
- packed internal dependency-version gate complete: `pnpm run verify:packages` now rejects internal `@blade-ai/*` dependency ranges in packed tarball manifests, allowing only the local-pack `0.0.0` placeholder or a concrete exact version. This keeps internal monorepo dependencies from drifting in tarballs while still respecting the fixed-version release prepare step.
- published package dependency-version gate complete: `pnpm run verify:published` now rejects external dependency ranges from the installed npm package manifests, while still requiring internal `@blade-ai/*` dependencies to match the released fixed version. This closes the dependency-version loop from source manifests to packed tarballs to actual user-installed artifacts.
- Dependency-version rule sharing complete: source boundary checks, packed-package verification, and post-publish verification now reuse `scripts/dependency-version-rules.mjs` for exact-version and internal `@blade-ai/*` dependency predicates. This keeps the source, tarball, and installed-artifact dependency gates aligned as the monorepo release path evolves.
- post-publish temporary consumer toolchain pin gate complete: the post-publish verifier now installs its temporary consumer TypeScript and esbuild tooling with exact versions instead of ranges, so published package type and browser-bundle smoke checks do not drift with npm resolution while validating a release.
- Twenty-sixth release-automation increment complete: the post-publish verifier now includes a published package manifest gate. After installing the released packages into a temporary consumer, `pnpm run verify:published -- --version <version>` reads each installed package manifest, rejects version mismatches, `workspace:` protocols, `0.0.0` placeholders, and internal `@blade-ai/*` dependency versions that do not match the released fixed version. This proves semantic-release manifest preparation is reflected in the npm-visible artifacts, not only in the source-tree dry run.
- Twenty-seventh release-automation increment complete: the post-publish verifier now includes a published package manifest entry target gate. After installing the released packages into a temporary consumer, it recursively checks installed `main`, `types`, and `exports` targets, allowing only `./dist/*` artifacts and the explicit `./package.json` metadata export while rejecting source-file targets, absolute/non-package-relative targets, and package escapes. This proves the npm-visible entrypoint map stays aligned with the built artifacts after publication.
- Published SDK browser export condition gate complete: the post-publish verifier now rejects published `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` manifests whose root browser condition no longer resolves to `./dist/browser/index.js`, or whose `server`, `session`, `session/internal`, and `local` browser conditions no longer resolve to the explicit server-only stub. The published SDK browser export condition gate catches npm artifact regressions that would otherwise drag server/local dependencies into browser consumers after publication.
- Twenty-eighth release-automation increment complete: the post-publish verifier now includes a published package file-scope gate. After installing the released packages into a temporary consumer, it scans each installed package directory and rejects declaration maps, JavaScript source maps, test files, and source files. This extends the packed-tarball file-scope rules to the actual npm-installed artifacts users consume.
- Twenty-ninth release-automation increment complete: the post-publish verifier now includes a published package npm metadata gate. After installing the released packages into a temporary consumer, it rejects npm-visible manifests whose `license`, `homepage`, `bugs`, or `repository` metadata drift from the source release contract, keeping published artifacts traceable and supportable after semantic-release preparation.
- published package repository directory metadata gate complete: source, packed-package, and post-publish manifest checks now require each publishable package `repository.directory` to match its `packages/*` workspace directory, so npm-visible package metadata points back to the correct monorepo source path for `@blade-ai/ai`, `@blade-ai/agent`, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk`.
- Thirtieth release-automation increment complete: `pnpm run verify:release` now keeps the workspace root as a private non-publishable orchestrator. The release gate rejects root `publishConfig` / `files` metadata and any semantic-release npm plugin configuration that would publish the workspace root, ensuring main-branch publishing can only target `@blade-ai/ai`, `@blade-ai/agent`, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk`.
- Thirty-first release-automation increment complete: the post-publish verifier now checks npm registry distribution metadata before trusting a release. `pnpm run verify:published -- --version <version>` requires each package's `latest` dist-tag to point at the released version, validates registry tarball `integrity` and `shasum` metadata, and cross-checks the temporary consumer `package-lock.json` resolved tarball URL and SRI integrity against the registry metadata.
- Thirty-second release-automation increment complete: `pnpm run verify:release` now requires the release workflow to serialize main-branch publishing through `concurrency.group: release-main` while keeping `cancel-in-progress: false`. This prevents overlapping semantic-release publish jobs without cancelling an in-flight npm publish or post-publish verifier.
- Thirty-third release-automation increment complete: the release workflow now upgrades trusted publishing with exact `npm@11.5.1`, and `pnpm run verify:release` rejects range-based npm CLI upgrades. This keeps the most sensitive publish-time tool pinned just like direct dependencies, pnpm, and the Node engine floor.
- Thirty-fourth release-automation increment complete: the trusted-publishing npm CLI upgrade now runs with `--ignore-scripts`, and `pnpm run verify:release` rejects npm CLI upgrade steps that would allow lifecycle scripts before package publication. This extends the workflow supply-chain policy beyond workspace installs to the publish-time tool bootstrap itself.
- Thirty-fifth release-automation increment complete: `pnpm run verify:release` now requires the release workflow checkout step to use `fetch-depth: 0`, preserving full git history and tags for semantic-release release notes, tag discovery, and post-publish version comparison.
- Release workflow shape gate complete: `pnpm run verify:release` now requires the release workflow to support manual `workflow_dispatch`, run on `ubuntu-latest`, keep a 20 minute timeout, use `actions/checkout@v5`, and keep `actions/setup-node` caching pnpm. This makes the main-branch publishing job shape explicit alongside permissions, concurrency, and command-order gates.
- source package version placeholder gate complete: all three publishable source package manifests now keep `version: "0.0.0"` until semantic-release prepares a concrete fixed-version release, and `pnpm run verify:release` rejects source version drift before main-branch publishing can run.
- CI least-privilege workflow permission gate complete: the CI workflow now grants only `contents: read`, and `pnpm run verify:release` rejects workflow drift that would let ordinary verification jobs inherit broader repository token permissions.
- CI workflow shape gate complete: `pnpm run verify:release` now requires CI to run on the expected main/master/refactor/codex branch pushes and pull requests, use `ubuntu-latest`, keep a 20 minute timeout, use `actions/checkout@v5`, and keep `actions/setup-node` caching pnpm. This makes the ordinary quality gate entrypoint as explicit as the release and docs workflows.
- Release workflow permission gate complete: `pnpm run verify:release` now requires the release workflow to grant exactly `contents: write`, `issues: write`, `pull-requests: write`, and `id-token: write`, preserving semantic-release/GitHub Release/trusted-publishing capability without allowing unrelated repository token scopes.
- Docs workflow permission gate complete: `pnpm run verify:release` now requires the docs deployment workflow to grant exactly `contents: read`, `pages: write`, and `id-token: write`, preserving GitHub Pages deployment while preventing broader repository token permissions.
- Docs workflow toolchain gate complete: `pnpm run verify:release` now also requires the docs deployment build job to pin `pnpm/action-setup` to `11.7.0`, run on Node `22`, execute only the frozen install plus `pnpm run docs:build`, and upload `docs/.vitepress/dist` as the Pages artifact. This keeps the production docs path covered by the same Pi-style workflow drift checks as CI and release.
- Docs workflow deployment gate complete: `pnpm run verify:release` now requires docs deployment to trigger only from `main` docs/workflow changes or manual dispatch, serialize through `concurrency.group: pages` without cancelling in-flight deploys, and run an `actions/deploy-pages@v4` deployment job against the `github-pages` environment only after the build job. This closes the docs publishing path beyond permissions, build tooling, and artifact location.
- npm-facing manifest hygiene gate complete: release preparation now removes `private` and `devDependencies` metadata from publishable package manifests, while `verify:release` and `verify:published` reject prepared or installed manifests that expose those fields. This keeps source workspace metadata separate from npm-facing package metadata.
- package license artifact gate complete: source, packed-package, and post-publish license verification now compares each package `LICENSE` artifact with the repository root `LICENSE` byte-for-byte after confirming the MIT permission grant, so npm-visible package metadata cannot drift from the source license. The README and production checklist document that source package LICENSE 与根 LICENSE 完全一致 and LICENSE 与根 LICENSE 完全一致.
- Agent public type contract drift gate complete: local entrypoint, packed-tarball, and post-publish temporary consumer type smokes now share their `@blade-ai/agent` public type import contract through `scripts/public-type-contracts.mjs`. This keeps `AgentKernelOptions`, `AgentStreamEvent`, `AgentToolCall`, `AgentToolPort`, and `AgentTraceEvent` pinned to explicit Pi-style subpaths across all verifier layers instead of relying on duplicated inline imports.
- AI public type contract drift gate complete: local entrypoint, packed-tarball, and post-publish temporary consumer type smokes now also share their `@blade-ai/ai` public type import contract through `scripts/public-type-contracts.mjs`. This keeps model, chat, retry, DeepSeek, OpenAI-compatible, and Vercel provider type imports aligned across verifier layers while leaving runtime value import smokes explicit in each consumer.
- SDK public type contract drift gate complete: local entrypoint, packed-tarball, and post-publish temporary consumer type smokes now share their `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` public type import contract through `scripts/public-type-contracts.mjs`. This keeps session-first root, core, session, tools, local, server, and browser declaration coverage aligned across verifier layers while preserving the runtime smoke tests in each package consumer.
- SDK errors browser-bundle verification complete: local entrypoint, packed-tarball, and post-publish temporary consumer browser bundle smokes now import and instantiate `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/errors` under browser conditions, proving the public `SdkError` / `ConfigError` runtime stays browser-safe across local dist, packed artifacts, and npm-installed packages.
- SDK errors source browser-safe boundary gate complete: `pnpm run verify:boundaries` now scans `packages/agent-sdk/src/errors` alongside `browser` and `core`, rejecting Node-only, MCP, server HTTP, local filesystem, and terminal imports before the browser-safe public error runtime reaches build or package smoke stages.
- SDK errors static-closure verification complete: local entrypoint, packed-tarball, and post-publish browser-safe static import closure gates now walk `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/errors` alongside browser and core, rejecting Node-only, MCP, local filesystem, terminal, and server runtime markers before and after packaging.
- SDK tools browser-safe static-closure verification complete: `pnpm run verify:boundaries` now scans `packages/agent-sdk/src/tools` alongside browser/core/errors, while local entrypoint, packed-tarball, and post-publish static import closure gates walk `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/tools` with the other browser-safe SDK entries.
- SDK tools browser-bundle verification complete: local entrypoint, packed-tarball, and post-publish temporary consumer browser bundle smokes now import `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/tools`, execute `defineTool()` and instantiate `ToolCatalog` under browser conditions, proving browser-safe tool authoring runtime stays usable without dragging local adapters into client bundles.
- SDK browser-safe export-condition verification complete: source, packed-tarball, and post-publish manifest gates now require `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` browser/core/errors/tools subpaths to declare explicit browser conditions pointing at their own browser-safe dist entries, while server/session/session-internal/local continue to resolve to the server-only browser stub.
- Pi-style pinned dependency gate complete: `pnpm run verify:boundaries` now rejects range-based `dependencies`, `devDependencies`, and `optionalDependencies` in the root orchestrator and publishable package source manifests, allowing only exact semver versions or `workspace:*`. This mirrors Pi's pinned-dependency discipline and keeps source-tree dependency drift from entering the build, pack, or release path.
- Source internal dependency workspace gate complete: `pnpm run verify:boundaries` now also rejects source manifests that declare internal `@blade-ai/*` dependencies with published version placeholders or exact versions instead of `workspace:*`. This keeps the local monorepo graph authoritative while release preparation remains responsible for rewriting package versions in npm-facing artifacts.

### Phase 7: Documentation and Examples

Objective: Make the new architecture clear for users and agents.

Deliverables:

- Architecture guide.
- Package guide.
- Session-first quickstart.
- Provider guide.
- Tool authoring guide.
- Server/Next.js guide.
- Browser-safe/remote-client guide.
- Production checklist.

Status:

- First documentation increment complete: VitePress now includes an architecture guide and package/entrypoint guide. The architecture guide documents the `@blade-ai/ai` / `@blade-ai/agent` / `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` responsibilities, dependency direction, runtime boundaries, observability boundaries, and production verification gates. The package guide documents recommended imports, Browser-safe vs Server / CLI boundaries, direct `@blade-ai/ai` provider usage, runtime-independent `@blade-ai/agent` usage, and session-first `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` usage.
- Second documentation increment complete: a session-first quickstart now documents the server/CLI `createSession()` flow, explicitly calls out `allowedTools: []` as disabling all tools, links to `examples/session-first-server.ts`, and adds `pnpm run verify:examples` to the root verification chain so the public quickstart import remains type-checked.
- Third documentation increment complete: a Server / Next.js guide now documents Route Handler and Server Action usage, keeps root `createSession()` server-only, requires `runtime = 'nodejs'` for Route Handlers, and tells browser clients to communicate over HTTP while importing only browser-safe `core` / `tools` contracts.
- Fourth documentation increment complete: a Browser Remote Client guide now documents browser-safe `core` / `browser` imports, remote HTTP NDJSON streaming with `StreamMessage`, server-only stub behavior, and the production boundary that keeps provider keys, tools, MCP, filesystem, shell, and sandbox execution on the server.
- Fifth documentation increment complete: a Production Checklist now documents the required `pnpm run verify` release gate, package boundary checks, browser-safe entrypoint checks, optional GLM live smoke, release dry-run, and trusted publishing flow from `main`.
- Sixth documentation increment complete: a Provider and model guide now documents session-first provider configuration, direct `@blade-ai/ai` `ModelPort` usage, OpenAI-compatible/GLM smoke testing, Vercel provider adapters, stream events, usage normalization, and the boundary that keeps `@blade-ai/agent` independent of provider SDKs.
- Seventh documentation increment complete: a Tool Authoring guide now documents production custom tool design for session-first apps, browser-safe tool imports, `defineTool` / `createTool`, `ExecutionContext`, allowed-tool policy, permission update effects, stream event mapping, and verification expectations.
- Eighth documentation increment complete: the root README now presents the Pi-style three-package architecture as installable public packages, documents the recommended `@blade-ai/ai` / `@blade-ai/agent` / `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` import boundaries, and surfaces the production verification chain including packed tarball temporary-consumer smoke, browser/entrypoint checks, default integration skip behavior, and explicit GLM live commands.
- Ninth documentation increment complete: examples now cover all three public package layers. `examples/ai-model-port.ts` demonstrates direct `@blade-ai/ai` `ModelPort.generate()` usage against OpenAI-compatible / GLM configuration, `examples/agent-kernel.ts` demonstrates runtime-independent `AgentKernel.runTurn()` with an injected model port, and `pnpm run verify:examples` type-checks those examples alongside the session-first server example.
- Tenth documentation increment complete: each publishable package README now includes a direct `pnpm add` command and minimal import usage for its public package. The package metadata contract test requires those install/import snippets to stay present before npm publication, keeping `@blade-ai/ai`, `@blade-ai/agent`, and `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` self-describing when viewed outside the monorepo.
- Eleventh documentation increment complete: the README, package guide, API reference, tools guide, recipes, MCP guide, and package README now document the Pi-style root/local split: root remains session-first for server and CLI code, while builtin tools, in-process MCP helpers, filesystem memory, and sandbox adapters must be imported from `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/local`.
- Twelfth documentation increment complete: the README, package guide, architecture guide, production checklist, provider guide, and package README now document the provider-helper split: `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` root remains session-first, while DeepSeek cost/cache/long-context/runtime helpers are imported from `@blade-ai/ai/deepseek`.
- Thirteenth documentation increment complete: the README and package guide now document `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/server` as an explicit server-only facade that composes session/core/tools/subagent exports without wildcard-forwarding through root, matching the packed and post-publish verifier guardrails.
- Fourteenth documentation increment complete: the README and package guide now clarify that `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/server` stays aligned with the root entry's server-safe runtime/type surface while remaining independently auditable as an explicit facade.
- Fifteenth documentation increment complete: the package-boundary examples now type-check `@blade-ai/agent/budget` through the examples path map, and `examples/agent-kernel.ts` demonstrates injecting `TokenBudget` plus handling `budget_warning` / `budget_exhausted` events. The package guide documents the explicit budget subpath alongside the runtime-independent `AgentKernel` import.
- Sixteenth documentation increment complete: root unit tests now include a docs browser/client import policy scanner. README, package README, and VitePress docs can continue showing session-first root imports for server/CLI sections, but browser/client fenced code sections must not recommend `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` root, server, session, or local imports.

- `docs: document production agent sdk architecture`

### Hardening: Verification Chain and Root/Local Split Enforcement

Objective: Fix verification chain breakage and enforce Pi-style root/local split.

Status:

- Fixed `packages/agent-sdk/src/local/packageInfo.ts` to walk up directory tree when resolving `package.json` instead of hardcoding `../../package.json`, making it robust against tsup bundling at different nesting levels. Previously the bundled `dist/index.js` would resolve `packages/package.json` (missing), causing `pnpm run verify:entrypoints` to crash with ENOENT.
- Removed local utility re-exports (`getVersion`, `getPackageName`, `getEnvironmentContext`, `getEnvironmentInfo`, `normalizePath`, `PathSecurity`, `checkRestricted`, `getRelativePath`, `isWithinWorkspace`, `validatePath`) from `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` root entry. These Node-dependent utilities now belong exclusively to `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/local`, matching the documented Pi-style root/local split.
- Added utility exports to `packages/agent-sdk/src/local/index.ts` (runtime source) and `packages/agent-sdk/src/local/public-index.ts` (public type declarations) so consumers can import them from `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/local`.
- Updated root legacy shims (`src/utils/pathSecurity.ts`, `src/utils/packageInfo.ts`, `src/utils/environment.ts`) to re-export from `@blade-ai/agent-sdk/local` instead of `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` root.
- Fixed `scripts/verify-entrypoints.mjs` and `scripts/verify-packages.mjs` to accept `isThinkingModel` as a valid singleton runtime export from `@blade-ai/ai/model`, replacing the overly-strict "must be type-only at runtime" assertion.
- Fixed `localMemoryTools.test.ts` expectation to match actual `getBuiltinTools()` behavior (returns both Read and Write tools by default).

Commit:

- `fix(agent-sdk): harden package.json resolution and enforce root/local split`

## Completion Criteria

The migration is complete only when all of the following are true:

- The repo has `packages/ai`, `packages/agent`, and `packages/agent-sdk`.
- `@blade-ai/agent-sdk` still supports session-first `createSession()`.
- `@blade-ai/agent` is proven free of Node-local runtime dependencies.
- `@blade-ai/ai` owns provider execution and usage normalization.
- Existing session, tools, hooks, observability, permission, and token budget behavior is covered by tests.
- CI verifies lint, type-check, build, unit, integration, package exports, browser safety, docs, and pack/install.
- Pushes to `main` can publish automatically after gates pass.
- Documentation describes the package boundaries and recommended usage.
